## Document 001: 10年期国债收益率创2002年最高，法国会是欧债危机2.0的引爆点吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/10年期国债收益率创2002年最高，法国会是欧债危机2.0的引爆点吗？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

法国财政恶化与社会动荡交织，债券市场⻛险警报持续升级： 10 年期国债收益率⼀度触及
5% ，创 2002 年以来新⾼，法德利差逼近 160 个基点。穆迪评级审查临近， 2027 年预算案
推进受阻，外国投资者加速撤离。分析⼈⼠警告，法国或成为欧元区新⼀轮主权债务危机
的⻛险源，财政紧缩与政治博弈也将考验欧洲整体稳定。
摘要：
法国正同时承受社会动荡与财政恶化的双重压⼒，债券市场已率先作出反应。
法国 10 年期国债收益率周三早间⼀度升⾄ 5% ，创 2002 年以来最⾼⽔平。
与此同时，法德 10 年期国债利差逼近 160 个基点，触及欧债危机以来最⾼点，外国投资者正加速
撤离法国国债市场。
市场的紧张情绪叠加多重催化剂：上周末爆发的极左翼团体骚乱、本周晚些时候即将到来的穆
迪主权评级审查，以及政府在推进 2027 年预算案上的持续受阻。德意志银⾏⾼级经济学家
Yacine Rouimi 在研究报告中指出，在 18 个欧元区成员国中，法国是⾯临主权债务危机⻛险最⾼
的经济体。
骚乱失控，内政部⻓请辞遭拒

上周末，法国多个⼤城市爆发由极左翼团体主导的街头骚乱，暴徒冲击并洗劫零售商店及奢侈
品⻔店，纵⽕焚烧建筑，并与警⽅发⽣激烈冲突。
内政部⻓ Laurent Nuñez 周⼀公开承认，警⽅在部分⼤都市区⼀度失去对局势的控制。 Nuñez 随
后向总统⻢克⻰提出辞职，但遭到后者拒绝。
花旗集团策略师 Jamie Searle 警告称， "OAT 与德债利差昨⽇已告破位 " ，法国的国别⻛险溢价正
进⼊ " 未知领域 " 。Searle 同时指出，上周末的骚乱进⼀步加剧了法国预算谈判的复杂程度。
财政积弊深重，评级压⼒迫近
法国的财政困境并⾮⼀朝⼀⼣。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
《世界报》编辑总监、英国《⾦融时报》专栏作家 Sylvie Kauffmann 撰⽂指出，法国⾃ 1974 年
以来从未实现财政平衡，新冠疫情后 " 不惜⼀切代价 " 的财政扩张政策更将公共⾚字推升⾄今年
GDP 的 5.4% 。⽬前，法国国家债务已达 GDP 的 119% ，为 1946 年以来最⾼⽔平，且仍在持续攀
升。
穆迪定于本周晚些时候对法国 Aa3 主权评级展开审查，该评级此前已被列⼊负⾯展望。在政治僵
局之下，⻢克⻰政府能否顺利通过 2027 年财年预算案，仍存在较⼤不确定性。债券市场的定价
已在相当程度上反映出对评级下调的担忧。
欧债危机 2.0 ⻛险浮现
德意志银⾏经济学家 Rouimi 构建的欧盟经济动荡⻛险评估框架涵盖公共财政、对外收⽀、私⼈
债务、银⾏业韧性及劳动⼒市场等多个维度。

评估结果显⽰，爱尔兰、希腊和葡萄⽛⾃ 2010 年以来⻛险状况显著改善，⽽法国、⽐利时和芬
兰则持续朝错误⽅向演变。 Rouimi 的结论是，⼀旦欧元区再度爆发系统性危机，法国可能成为
新的震源。
花旗策略师 Searle 亦对此表⽰担忧。外国投资者持续减持法国国债的趋势，正在加剧市场对流动
性和融资成本的顾虑。
福利削减难题，⼤选前景⽣变
分析⼈⼠指出，此轮骚乱的深层逻辑与法国福利体系的可持续性危机密切相关。在⾼利率环境
下，如何为庞⼤的福利国家持续融资，已成为法国财政政策的核⼼⽭盾。若不推⾏实质性财政
整顿，投资者信⼼的进⼀步流失将难以避免。
然⽽，任何紧缩措施都将⾯临来⾃左翼的强烈抵制，⽽这恰恰为极右翼候选⼈ Marine Le Pen 在
明年总统⼤选中提供了有利的⺠调空间。当前的多重危机，正在重塑法国政治格局，并对欧元
区整体稳定构成潜在威胁。

## Document 019: 2026-10-15_［みずほ経済インサイト］金利上昇による企業の負担増加はいつ本格化するか_eaa924d1

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-15_［みずほ経済インサイト］金利上昇による企業の負担増加はいつ本格化するか_eaa924d1.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

金利上昇による企業の負担増加は 
いつ本格化するか

○ 日銀の政策金利や、金融市場で決まる国債金利に比べ、企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇はまだ
限定的。企業の新規借入金利や負債残高全体への影響波及に時間がかかることが背景 
○ 金融機関の新規貸出金利は、概ね3カ月かけて政策金利の変化を反映する傾向。特に地域金融機関
で上昇がやや遅く、企業規模により有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが異なる一因に 
○ 借入・社債金利の動きを踏まえると、企業の利払い負担増加は2026～2028年度に本格化し、年間
約3兆円の利益下押し要因に。これまで以上に資本効率を意識した経営が求められる

１．政策金利や国債金利が上昇するも、企業の利払い負担増加はまだ本格化せず 
日本銀行は2026年9月の金融政策決定会合で、 政策金利を1.0％から1.25％へ引き上げた。これは1995
年以来、31年ぶりの高水準である1。また、金融市場で決まる10年国債金利（いわゆる長期金利）は、
2026年9月に節目となる3％を超えた。こちらも1996年以来30年ぶりの高さであり、政策金利や国債金
利はまさに歴史的な転換点を迎えている。 
一方で、 企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇は今のところ限定的だ。 図表1は、 企業の有利子負債利子
率 （＝支払利息等÷有利子負債残高） を指標金利 （政策金利、10年国債金利）と比較したものである。
図表 1 企業の有利子負債利子率と指標金利 図表2 企業の資金繰り判断 DI

平均値で除した値、対象は金融・保険を除く全産業・

図中の凡例にある数字は、 各金利についてマイナス金利政策解除 （2024年3月19日 ）前 の2023年10～12
月期と、直近の2026年4～6月期を比較した値だ。政策金利が＋0.93％Pt、10年国債金利が＋1.80％Pt
と大きく上昇した一方で、企業の有利子負債利子率は＋0.42％Ptと上昇幅が小さく、企業の利払い負
担の増加はまだ本格化していない。実際、日銀短観における企業の資金繰りに関する調査（図表2）を
みると、「楽である」の回答率から「苦しい」の回答率を差し引いたDIが、2021年以降プラス圏で概
ね横ばいを維持している。企業の実感としても、これまでに実施された複数回の利上げや、金融市場
における国債金利の大幅な上昇は、まだ資金調達環境の悪化につながっていないようだ。 
なぜ、こうしたギャップが生じるのか。主な要因として考えられるのは次の2点である。 
第一の要因は、指標金利と企業の新規調達金利とのタイムラグだ。政策金利などの指標金利が変化
してから、企業が新たに資金を調達する際の金利が変化するまで、少し時間がかかる。特に、企業の
有利子負債の半分強を占める金融機関からの借入において（図表3）、新規調達金利のタイムラグが生
じやすいと考えられる。 
第二の要因は、企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグだ。企業が
新たに資金調達する際の金利が上昇しても、それが有利子負債残高全体にすぐ反映されるわけではな
い。 企業の金融機関借入金の4～5割程度が固定金利型であるほか2、 主に大企業が発行する社債の多く
が固定利付債であり、過去の低金利時代に調達した負債がまだ残っているためだ。 
本稿では、 これら2つの要因を踏まえて、政策金利・国債金利などの指標金利上昇を受けた企業の利
払い負担増加が、いつ本格的に現れるかを試算する3。 
２．金融機関からの新規借入金利は、地域金融機関で上昇ペースが遅い傾向 
まず、第一の要因（指標金利から企業の新規調達金利へのタイムラグ）の影響を検証しよう。図表
3に示したように、 企業の有利子負債には金融機関などからの借入金と、 主に大企業が発行する社債が
ある。このうち、社債を新規発行する際の金利は概ね国債金利に連動して決まり、タイムラグがほぼ

含まれるが、 シェアが小さい （0.1％程度） ため表示し

無いため、ここでの検証は金融機関などから新規に借り入れる際の金利が対象になる。 
図表4は、金融機関の新規貸出金利（金融機関からみた貸出）を、業態別に示したものだ。都市銀行
では新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが速い一方、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では政策金利の上昇に比べて新
規貸出金利の上昇ペースがやや遅い。こうした地域金融機関における金利上昇の遅れが、指標金利と
企業の新規調達金利のタイムラグに影響していると考えられる。 
金融機関の業態によって新規貸出金利の上昇ペースに差があるのは、貸出の金利形態別シェアが異
なることが一因であろう。大手行では企業向け貸出全体の約6割を占める変動金利型貸出のうち、ほと
んどが「市場金利連動」であるため、金融市場の変化による影響がすぐに反映されやすい。他方で、
地方銀行や信用金庫では変動金利型貸出のうち「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが高い。短期プ
ライムレートとは、各金融機関が設定する企業向け貸出の最優遇金利である。政策金利の変更などを
反映して決められることが多いが、各金融機関の競争環境などによって反映時期・度合いに差が生じ
る4。これにより、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では都市銀行に比べて新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが遅くな
る傾向があると考えられる5。 
こうした金融機関業態別の違いは、企業サイドにおいて、有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが規模別
に異なる一因になっている可能性がある。足元の金利上昇局面では、都市銀行からの借入が多いと考
えられる大企業（資本金10億円以上）で有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが速く、信用金庫など地域金
融機関からの借入が多いと考えられる小規模企業（同1千万円未満）で上昇ペースが遅い（図表5）。
2010年代の金利低下局面では大企業の有利子負債利子率が小規模企業を下回る傾向にあったが、足元
では完全に逆転している。大企業は社債が有利子負債残高の約3分の1を占めるため、金融機関の新規
貸出金利のみが原因とは言えないものの、金融機関業態別の違いが企業規模別の資金調達環境に影響
を及ぼしている可能性がある点は興味深い。 
では、今後日銀がさらに利上げを進めていくと、金融機関の新規貸出金利はどの程度のペースで、
どこまで上昇していくか。過去の政策金利と新規貸出金利の関係を統計的に分析すると、 政策金利1％

### Page 4

Ptの上昇は新規貸出金利を当月に0.31％Pt押し上げ、その後さらに3カ月間かけて当月からの累計で
0.85％Pt押し上げる傾向があることが明らかになった。日銀が今後2027 年半ばにかけて政策金利を
2.0％まで引き上げていくことを前提にすると6、金融機関の新規貸出金利（2026年8月時点で1.78％）
は2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度へ上昇すると試算される （図表6） 。 マイナス金利政策の解除前と
比較すると、2％Pt弱の上昇になる計算だ。 
３．企業の利払い負担増加は今後 2028 年度にかけて本格化。 利益を年間約 3 兆円下押し 
次に、第二の要因（企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグ）の影
響を検証しよう。金融機関の新規貸出金利や社債の新規発行金利の上昇が、それぞれの残高全体にど
の程度のペースで反映されるかを確認し、企業の利払い負担がいつ本格化に増加するか試算する。 
金融機関の新規貸出金利と貸出残高全体の金利を比較すると、今回の金利上昇局面では新規貸出金
の上昇に対して貸出金利残高全体の金利上昇がやや遅れている（図表7）。 先に述べたように、金融機
関の貸出の一部が固定金利型であり、新規貸出や借り換えに伴って新規貸出金利の上昇が残高全体へ
波及するのに時間がかかるためだ。 
両者の過去の関係を統計的に分析すると、新規貸出金利の上昇がおよそ5年程度かけて残高全体の金
利を緩やかに押し上げる傾向があるとの結果になった。 図表6で試算したように、 先行きの新規貸出金
利が2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度まで上昇していくと想定すると、貸出残高全体の金利（2026年
8月時点で1.51％ ）は2027年末に2.0％程度、2028年末に2.3％程度と緩やかに上昇し、2030年代前半に
は新規貸出金利と同じ2.65％程度へ収束していく計算になる。 
同様に社債について、 新発債金利と既発債金利を比較したものが図表8だ。 新発債金利は2022年ごろ
から上昇して足元では2％台後半に達しているが、既発債金利はまだ1％強にとどまっており、金融機
関の貸出金利に比べると残高全体への反映に時間がかかりやすいことがうかがえる。なお、日本で新

### Page 5

5

規に発行される社債の平均年限は現在5～6年であり、新発債金利は概ね5 年国債金利に連動して動く
傾向があることから、先に述べたように指標金利→新発債金利のタイムラグはほとんど生じない。 
今後、社債残高が物価上昇にあわせて年間＋2％のペースで増加していくとの前提を置き、 先行きの
償還に伴う借り換えや追加的な新規発行が行われていく場合、既発債金利は2030年代初めにかけて年
間＋0.3％Pt程度の緩やかなペースで上昇していくと試算される（新発債金利は5年国債金利の先行き
予測値に連動すると想定）。7年債や10年債といった年限が長めの社債も一定の規模があることから、
新発債金利の上昇が既発債金利に反映されるまでに時間を要する結果になった。 
最後に、金融機関の貸出金利（企業からみた借入金利7）と社債金利の試算結果を用いて、企業の有
利子負債利回りへの影響を検証する。企業の有利子負債に占める借入と社債の金額シェアを先行き一
定として、 貸出金利と社債金利の変化を有利子負債利子率に反映したものが図表9だ。2023～2025年度
の有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースは年間＋0.1～＋0.2％Pt程度と緩やかだったが、2026～2028年度に
は同＋0.3～＋0.4％Ptに加速すると見込まれる。企業の有利子負債の多くを占める借入金の金利上昇
が、主な押し上げ要因だ。2029年以後は借入金の金利上昇が落ち着く一方、社債金利の上昇継続を受
け、有利子負債利子率は2030年代にかけて緩やかに3％程度の水準まで上昇していくだろう。 
このように、金利上昇による企業の利払い負担増加は今後2028年度にかけて本格化していく見込み
だ。企業全体の有利子負債残高が先行き年間＋2％のペースで拡大していくと仮定すると、 支払利息等
が年間約3兆円増加する計算であり、経常利益（金融・保険を除くベースで2025年度：124.8兆円）に
対し年間2.4％の押し下げ要因に相当する8。 
今後、有利子負債利子率が上昇していく過程では、それに見合った利益率を達成できるかが問われ
るだろう。2025年度時点では投下資本利益率（ROIC、3.70％）の有利子負債利子率（1.45％）に対す
るスプレッドが2％Ptを上回っており、ROICでみた企業の資本効率は外部資金調達コストを十分カバー
できている（図表10）9。しかし、 有利子負債利子率が今後3％程度へ上昇していくと、ROICが現在の水
準のままではスプレッドがコロナ禍初期の2020年度並みに縮小し、企業の利払い負担に対する利益の

余力が弱まってしまう。日本の金利上昇が一段と進み、企業の利払い負担が本格的に増加していくと

［参考文献］ 
みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）「2026年・2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ～転換点は近いのか、 投資
主導経済の行方を探る～」、2026年10月2日

1 現在の政策金利は無担保コール翌日物金利の誘導目標だが、1995 年時点では公定歩合を政策金利と見なして時系列比較してお
り、厳密には政策金利の意味するものが異なる。 
2 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2026年4 月）の図表Ⅲ-1-12 によれば、法人向け貸出のうち、大手行で約40％、地域銀
行（地方銀行、第二地方銀行）や信用金庫で 45～50％程度が固定金利貸出である（2025 年 9 月末時点）。 
3 金利上昇による企業への影響には、利払いの増加によるデメリットだけでなく、利子収入の増加によるメリットもある。本稿
では、そのうちデメリットの面だけに焦点を当てて分析しており、メリットについては稿を改めて論じたい。 
4 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2025年4 月）の図表Ⅵ-1-8 では、地域銀行を対象に、2024 年7 月の政策金利上昇を受け
た短期プライムレートの引き上げタイミング、引き上げ幅、引き上げ後金利の貸出先適用割合について分析が行われている。 
5 図表 4 に示した金融機関業態別の新規貸出金利には、企業向け以外の貸出も含まれる。したがって、企業向け貸出における
「市場金利連動」 、 「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが業態別に異なることだけでなく、貸出全体に占める住宅ローン貸出
のシェアが業態別に異なることなども、新規貸出金利の上昇ペースの違いに影響していると考えられる。 
6 みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）の見通しに基づく想定。 
7 金融機関の貸出金利には企業向け以外の貸出も含まれるが、ここでは簡易的に、企業向け貸出金利が貸出全体の金利に連動す
ると仮定して試算した。 
8 本稿では金利上昇による利払いの増加のみを計算対象としているため、利子収入の増加も考慮すれば経常利益への下押し圧力
は緩和すると考えられる。 
9 投下資本利益率（ROIC）と本来比較すべきは、負債だけでなく自己資本のコストも考慮した加重平均資本コスト（WACC）だ
が、本稿では金利上昇の影響を直接評価する観点から、投下資本利益率と有利子負債利子率を比べている。

市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。

顧 • AI安全監管升溫，短期壓抑市場情緒：Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度，並引入獨立第三方
評估等安全機制，引發市場對AI資本支出降溫的疑慮。不過，前沿模型開發速度放緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。
Alphabet、微軟及亞馬遜近期AI相關積壓訂單持續攀升，合計達約1.64兆美元，其中微軟及Alphabet分別突破6,000億及
5,000億美元，搭配雲端業務持續成長，顯示企業AI需求仍具韌性，相關訂單有望逐步轉化為未來數年的營收與獲利。
• 槓桿壓力逐步消化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值：美國雲端巨頭擴大AI資本支出，壓抑短期自由現金流；相較之下，亞洲
科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。
新興市場本幣債券各因子對報酬率貢獻
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

## Document 217: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

## Document 231: AI算力够不够？硅谷喊“供不应求”，华尔街警告“价格崩塌”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI算力够不够？硅谷喊“供不应求”，华尔街警告“价格崩塌”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 算⼒投资陷叙事分裂：⻢斯克称算⼒严重短缺、回本周期或压缩⾄六个⽉， 2027 年拟新
增 10GW ；⽪查伊确认客⼾锁定 2027 年算⼒极难，需求预测已系统性失效。 Citadel 创始⼈
Griffin 则警告 GPU 边际成本极低，供过于求将致现货价格⾃由落体，折旧模型失效与企业
ROI 收紧⻛险浮现，市场重新定价才刚开始。
摘要：
AI 算⼒投资正站在叙事分裂的⼗字路⼝。硅⾕的掌舵者们以实际订单和极短回本周期为证据，坚
称当前算⼒严重短缺、供不应求；⽽华尔街的顶级资管⼈则冷静地追问：⼀旦供给赶上需求，
边际定价崩塌、折旧模型失效、企业端 ROI 精算收紧，这⻔⽣意的回报还能否维系？
SpaceX ⾸席执⾏官⻢斯克在最新⼀季财报电话会上抛出了迄今最激进的算⼒扩张计划 —— 预计
2026 年底在线算⼒达到 2.5 ⾄ 3GW ， 2027 年再新增 10GW ，并以 " 如果届时上线算⼒低于
13GW ，我会感到意外 " 来定调业绩指引。他描述的现货市场图景是：所有超⼤规模云⼚商和数

⼗家中⼩公司正 " ⽆情地围追堵截 " ，要求租⽤更多算⼒， " 如果我们对所有⼈都说 ' 好 ' ，我们的算
⼒可以出租好⼏次 " 。⾕歌 CEO ⽪查伊（ Sundar Pichai ）同样罕⻅地公开确认，客⼾今天想锁定
2027 年算⼒ " 极其困难 " ，全⾏业都希望⼿⾥的算⼒是当初规划量的两倍。
然⽽， Citadel 创始⼈ Ken Griffin 却在彭博采访中发出警告：GPU 与航空公司⾼度同构，边际运
⾏成本极低，⼀旦供过于求，现货租赁价格将 " ⾃由落体 " ，当前市场正从 " 主题式扫货 " 转向真正
的基本⾯研究，⽽这次检验 " 才刚刚开始 " 。
这场分歧的核⼼，不仅是对 AI 需求曲线的判断差异，更是对 " 算⼒价值 " 本质的根本性分歧 —— 是
稀缺资源的溢价，还是最终将商品化的基础设施？
⻢斯克：回本周期将压缩⾄六个⽉，算⼒需求远未⻅顶
SpaceX 第三季度财报提供了当前 AI 算⼒供需失衡最直观的数据切⾯。截⾄ 9 ⽉的 2026 财年第三
季度，公司营收同⽐增⻓ 161% ⾄ 145.8 亿美元，超出分析师预期约 10% 。其中超级智能（ SI ）

业务单季营收达 76 亿美元，环⽐激增 50 亿美元，成为最核⼼的增⻓引擎。公司净利润从去年同
期亏损 11 亿美元转为盈利 44 亿美元，调整后 EBITDA 同⽐增⻓ 306% ⾄ 88 亿美元。
⻢斯克在财报电话会上描述的商业逻辑直指需求强度：超算基础设施的部署成本回收周期已压
缩⾄不⾜⼀年，⽽⾸席财务官 Bret Johnsen 则表⽰，以现⾏定价⽔平，每⽡价格已⾼于此前讨论
的 30 ⾄ 50 美元区间上限，回本周期不⾜⼀年。⻢斯克的表述更为激进：
" 不排除明年算⼒的投资回收期缩短⾄六个⽉。 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻢斯克选择按现货市场定价签订短期合同，⽽⾮锁定⻓期协议，其底层逻辑是对算⼒单位价值
上⾏趋势的判断：
在部署节奏上，⻢斯克透露团队⽬前每⽉稳定上线约 20 万块 GB300 ，明年将延续这⼀节奏并切
换⾄ Vera Rubin 算⼒。他同时确认，将于第⼆季度末完成 44 万台 Vera Rubin 组成的训练集群上
线。 SpaceX 第四季度资本⽀出预计将达 350 亿⾄ 400 亿美元，公司维持今年年底 SI 业务年度经常
性收⼊（ ARR ）达 1000 亿美元的⽬标，并预测 2027 年底 ARR 将介于 5000 亿⾄ 1 万亿美元之间。
⽪查伊：云业务正为前沿 AI 研发让渡算⼒，需求预测模型已系统性失效
⾕歌 CEO ⽪查伊在接受《 Big Technology Podcast 》专访时，提供了⼀个来⾃ AI ⽣态另⼀极的佐
证。他确认，⾕歌旗舰模型 Gemini 4 Argon 发布后 72 ⼩时内消耗了 25 万亿 Token ，是上⼀代
Gemini 3.0 同期的三倍。他援引的内部数据同样具体： Argon 上线四周内，⾕歌内部代码⽣产量

提升 30% ，代码回滚率下降约三分之⼀。
在算⼒短缺的判断上，⽪查伊直接确认了业界流传的说法合理性 ——客⼾今天想锁定 2027 年算
⼒ " 极其困难 " 。他将这⼀问题归结于基础设施建设的刚性时滞：
" 随着 AI 软件持续进步、模型不断优化，每⽡算⼒能产⽣的有效智能价值也在提升，这是算⼒定
价的根本⽀撑。从合同续签情况来看，我们看到的是每⽡美元单价在上升，⽽⾮下降。 "

### Page 3

更罕⻅的是，⽪查伊公开承认了⾕歌内部算⼒资源的优先级排序：前沿 AI 研发是 " 不可谈判 " 的第
⼀优先级，云业务在实际运营中 " 已经在为⽀持前沿 AI 开发⽽放弃短期收⼊，因为我们在做算⼒
上的取舍 " 。这是⼀个 CEO 级别的正式确认，⽽⾮战略展望。
对于 " 过度建设 " 的质疑，⽪查伊的回应是区分时间框架： " 如果拉⾜够⻓的时间框架，这个量级
的建设完全合理。我们处于有史以来最重要的技术的早期阶段，这不像光纤 —— 光纤铺完是暗
的，没⼈⽤。我们是在每天都⾯临产能约束的情况下使⽤它。 " 他并未否认短期内存在局部过剩
的可能，但对⻓期需求的判断是确定性的： " 给⾜时间的话，我们规划的算⼒最终会有⽤武之
地。 "
Griffin ：三⼤陷阱直指算⼒投资逻辑根基
在 Citadel 年度全球市场未来峰会上， Ken Griffin 接受彭博记者 Dani Burger 采访时，从三个维度
系统性地质疑了硅⾕叙事的可持续性。
边际定价⻛险是 Griffin 的第⼀层警告。他将 GPU 与航空业直接类⽐： "GPU 和航空公司⾮常相

似，边际运⾏成本极低。航班上有⼀个空座，哪怕只卖 20 美元，那个座位也是赚钱的。正是这
⼀特性决定了，⼀旦出现运⼒过剩，定价就会⾃由落体。 " 他援引 2000 年电信泡沫中 " 暗光纤 " 的
覆辙作为历史参照 —— ⼤量已铺设的光纤因需求不⾜从未被点亮，电信价格随之直线下跌。在
他的框架中，当前市场的⾼现货价格并⾮需求韧性的证明，⽽是供给仍处收紧阶段的暂时性溢
价。
折旧模型失效是第⼆层⻛险。 Griffin 指出，当前市场存在⼀个异常现象：五六年前的⽼旧 GPU 依
然能收取极⾼租⾦。但他认为这种状态难以持续： " 越来越多的⼈开始担忧，我不能再像 12 个⽉
前那样，对⼀块芯⽚的第七、⼋、九年给予同样的资本化率或收⼊模型。 " 若投资机构仍按 7 ⾄ 9
年⾼残值进⾏折旧贴现，其估值模型将⾯临系统性重估压⼒。
企业端 ROI 精算是第三层结构性压⼒。 Griffin 描述了⼀个与⼤规模资本开⽀叙事相悖的企业侧图
景： " 美国最精明的企业，正在变得⾮常清醒 —— 哪些业务需要为顶尖模型⽀付极⾼价格，哪些

业务可以⽤开源模型以极低成本运⾏。整个企业界都在看着来⾃ Anthropic 和 OpenAI 的账单，
然 后 说 ： 我 们 需 要 想 清 楚 ， 怎 么 确 保 真 的 从 中 获 得 了 ⽣ 产 ⼒ 。" 据 《 华 尔 街 ⽇ 报 》 报 道 ，
Anthropic 在其 S-1 ⽂件中披露，未来计划投⼊ 7500 亿美元算⼒开⽀，且⽀出规模仍在增加。记
者就此追问 Griffin ，两件事之间是否存在张⼒，他的回答简短⽽直接： " 是的。你在提问的时候
就已经回答了这个问题。 "
"数据中⼼从决策到投产有 2 ⾄ 3 年的滞后，⽽我们今天⼿⾥有的算⼒，是 2024 年做的决策的产
物。就算回到去年今天，智能体任务的爆发还没有发⽣。 "

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
171 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
Griffin 并不否认 AI 技术的变⾰性，他表⽰： " 在这个时刻，⼏乎可以肯定存在局部泡沫。任何对
经济的根本性变⾰，通常都与泡沫交织在⼀起。 " 但他强调，这不意味着当下的定价⻛险可以被
忽视 ——" 市场的重新定价才刚刚开始 " 。
分歧的本质：时间框架与定价基础的双重错位
梳理三⽅表述，这场分歧并⾮简单的乐观与悲观之争，⽽是建⽴在不同时间框架和不同定价假
设上的平⾏叙事。
⻢斯克和⽪查伊描述的，是⼀个当下正在发⽣的、由智能体爆发驱动的即时需求冲击 —— 算⼒
⽆论如何堆叠都不够⽤，回本周期以⽉计⽽⾮以年计，现货价格持续⾛⾼。这套逻辑的成⽴前
提，是需求增速的持续性将压倒供给的追赶速度。
Griffin 质疑的，是这⼀ " 稀缺溢价 " ⼀旦供给完成追赶后能否维系的问题。他的核⼼命题不是否认

当前需求的真实性，⽽是指出 GPU 作为资产类别在结构上接近航空座位 —— 边际成本趋近于零
意味着⼀旦均衡被打破，价格发现将以⾮线性的⽅式向下调整。
两套叙事都有内在⾃洽性，但针对的是不同的时间截⾯。当前的市场张⼒，在于投资者需要判
断：算⼒的稀缺窗⼝究竟还有多⻓，⽽这⼀判断对超算基础设施提供商、 GPU ⼚商及相关供应
链的估值影响，将是量级性的差异。

## Document 277: CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-09-22

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn(SCS) 
Thách thức từ Long Thành 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS từ 2H2026

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản lượng 
và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, triển vọng 
dài hạn phụ thuộc khả năng tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga mới

Cổ tức 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 
VND/cổ phiếu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang svck). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 tỷ 
VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự suy 
giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng mùa cao điểm hàng hóa cuối năm và mạng bay Trung Đông 
phục hồi sẽ hỗ trợ sản lượng SCS trong những tháng cuối năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ 
cải thiện còn hạn chế do nhu cầu vận tải trên các tuyến Trung Đông – châu Á và 
châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn suy yếu. Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự 
kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy).

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sau khi các hạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành được hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay quốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân Sơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh khoảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân bổ hàng quốc tế tại TP. Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 10%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì cổ tức tiền mặt cao nhờ khả năng tạo dòng 
tiền ổn định và quy mô tiền gửi lớn. Cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ 
phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027 nhằm tích lũy 
nguồn lực cho các dự án mới.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ hàng hóa Sài Gòn được thành 
lập năm 2008, hiện là đơn vị khai thác nhà ga 
hàng hóa hàng không lớn thứ 2 tại cảng 
hàng không quốc tế (HKQT) Tân Sơn Nhất 
với thị phần khoảng 50%. Các cổ đông lớn 
của SCS bao gồm CTCP Gemadept (33.4%) 
và Tổng Công ty Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam 
(13.6%). 
Hoạt động khai thác nhà ga là mảng kinh 
doanh chính của SCS. Hiện SCS đang tiếp 
tục mua sắm trang thiết bị nâng công suất 
xử lý hàng lên 350,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy) dù đã được hỗ 
trợ bởi mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi và mùa cao điểm cuối năm 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Thông lượng hàng hóa quốc tế qua SCS bắt đầu giảm từ 2027 với 10%/35%/55%/70% hàng quốc tế 
Tân Sơn Nhất phân bổ sang cho L TA vào 2027/2028/2029/2030, trước khi bắt đầu tăng trưởng trở lại từ 
2031. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định nhờ nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh, cổ tức 2026 
dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu và duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027.

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang yoy). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 
tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự 
suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

### Page 4

Biểu đồ 4. Cơ cấu thông lượng hàng SCS (tấn)  Biểu đồ 5. Giá dịch vụ và biên LNG hoạt động khai thác nhà ga 
của SCS

Nguồn: SCS  Nguồn: SCS

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS trong 2H2026, 
song mức độ cải thiện còn hạn chế 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng hàng hóa của SCS sẽ cải thiện trong những tháng 
cuối năm 2026 nhờ mạng bay Trung Đông dần được khôi phục, đặc biệt từ 
Qatar Airways, cùng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa tăng trong mùa cao điểm cuối 
năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ phục hồi dự kiến còn hạn chế khi nhu cầu vận tải trên 
các tuyến Trung Đông – Châu Á và Châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn giảm lần lượt 
14.1% và 16.1% yoy trong tháng 7/2026 (tháng thứ 5 liên tiếp), trái ngược với 
mức tăng 3.9% của thị trường hàng hóa hàng không toàn cầu. Diễn biến này 
phần nào giải thích việc sản lượng hàng quốc tế của SCS tiếp tục giảm khoảng 
10% yoy trong tháng 7–8, dù tần suất bay của các hãng Trung Đông đã phục 
hồi đáng kể.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng hàng quốc tế và nội địa trong bốn tháng cuối 
năm lần lượt giảm 4% và 2% yoy, cả năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy) và 
52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy). Doanh thu khai thác nhà ga được kỳ vọng tiếp tục 
được hỗ trợ bởi việc điều chỉnh tăng giá dịch vụ từ đầu năm, bù đắp một phần 
tác động từ sản lượng suy giảm.

Biểu đồ 6. Mức độ phục hồi chuyến bay của các hãng hàng không vùng Vịnh 
Đến ngày 21/09/2026, tần suất bay của các 
hãng vùng Vịnh đã phục hồi đáng kể, phần 
lớn đạt khoảng 85–100% mức trước xung đột, 
riêng Qatar Airways đạt 97%, tăng từ mức 
87% ghi nhận giữa tháng 6.

### Page 5

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản 
lượng và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, 
triển vọng dài hạn phụ thuộc khả 
năng tham gia vận hành nhà ga mới 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa hàng không khu vực phía Nam duy 
trì tăng trưởng trong trung hạn, với sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa tăng 8%/4% 
yoy năm 2027 và 9%/6% mỗi năm từ 2028, được hỗ trợ bởi hoạt động xuất 
nhập khẩu, thương mại điện tử và sự phát triển của chuỗi cung ứng. Tuy nhiên, 
sản lượng hàng quốc tế qua SCS dự kiến chịu áp lực khi các chuyến bay từng 
bước chuyển sang Long Thành. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi giả định tỷ lệ 
chuyến bay quốc tế chuyển sang Long Thành lần lượt đạt 10%/35%/55%/70% 
trong giai đoạn 2027–2030, với tác động lên sản lượng thực tế phụ thuộc vào 
tiến độ khai thác nhà ga hàng hóa và hệ thống logistics tại sân bay mới.

Đồng thời, giá dịch vụ quốc tế dự kiến bắt đầu giảm từ giữa năm 2027 nhằm giữ 
chân các hãng hàng không, trong khi giá nội địa kỳ vọng duy trì tăng 3%/năm. 
Theo đó, biên lợi nhuận gộp dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong năm 2027 và chịu áp lực 
đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ trọng hàng quốc tế có mức giá dịch vụ cao suy giảm.

Bên cạnh đó, SCS có cơ hội mở rộng quy mô khai thác trong dài hạn thông qua 
việc tham gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành, công suất thiết kế 
550 nghìn tấn/năm. Doanh nghiệp đã ký biên bản ghi nhớ với ACV về khả năng 
tham gia vận hành nhà ga này, qua đó có thể tiếp cận nguồn hàng dịch chuyển 
từ Tân Sơn Nhất nếu được lựa chọn. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu 
từ dự án vào kịch bản cơ sở khi chưa có kết quả lựa chọn đơn vị vận hành chính 
thức.

Biểu đồ 7. Thông lượng hàng và biên LNG của SCS 2022 – 2028F

Nguồn: SCS, KBSV dự phóng

Nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh và 
chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định 
hỗ trợ định giá cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định trong 
những năm tới nhờ khả năng tạo dòng tiền cao, quy mô tiền gửi lớn và mức 
đòn bẩy tài chính thấp. Trong 1H2026, thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh đã bù đắp 
phần lớn sự suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga, giúp LNST duy 
trì ổn định ở mức 361 tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy). Chúng tôi dự phóng cổ tức tiền mặt 
năm 2026 tiếp tục đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 
VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027, phù hợp với triển vọng lợi nhuận chịu áp lực từ 
Long Thành và nhu cầu tích lũy nguồn lực tài chính để tham gia các dự án mới.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền (DCF), trên cơ sở cập nhật triển 
vọng kinh doanh và tác động từ quá trình chuyển đổi khai thác sang Long 
Thành, chúng tôi đưa ra giá mục tiêu cho SCS là 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương với mức tiềm năng sinh lời 14.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 
22/09/2026. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu từ việc tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành vào định giá. Các giả định 
chính bao gồm: 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc tế năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 4% yoy do mạng bay Trung Đông chưa phục 
hồi hoàn toàn. Nhu cầu hàng quốc tế khu vực phía nam dự kiến tăng 
8% năm 2027 và 9%/năm trong giai đoạn 2028–2030, kéo theo lượng 
hàng qua SCS trong 2027/2028/2029/2030 sẽ giảm lần lượt -2.8%/-
21%/-25%/-27% yoy, trước khi tăng trưởng trở lại từ 2031 sau khi đạt 
tỷ trọng phân bổ tối ưu giữa SGN và LTA. 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc nội năm 2026 đạt 52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 2% yoy do áp lực chi phí vận tải và điều 
chỉnh mạng bay. Sản lượng dự kiến phục hồi 4% năm 2027 và 6%/năm 
từ 2028 khi chi phí nhiên liệu hạ nhiệt và năng lực khai thác cải thiện. 
- Giá nội địa dự kiến tăng 3%/năm, trong khi giá quốc tế bắt đầu giảm 
từ giữa 2027 nhằm giữ chân các hãng hàng không. Biên lợi nhuận gộp 
dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong 2027 và chịu áp lực đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ 
trọng hàng quốc tế suy giảm và chi phí cố định không giảm tương ứng

## Document 320: India_ Degrees of rupee defence (2026-10-16)

Source PDF: all report pdf/India_ Degrees of rupee defence (2026-10-16).pdf
Report source: DBS
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

although the circumstances 
differ. 
• Rupee gains have been 
accompanied with distortions 
elsewhere. 
• Authorities are likely not done 
and remain ready to take 
further action, if needed. 
• Scope of further rate

or inter-meeting move might

### Page 2

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

### Page 7

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            7

ECONOMICS & STRATEGY

望，透露部分客戶已洽詢2029年至2030年設
備訂單，顯示晶片擴產需求仍強 。台積電3Q

量與海外廠產能利用率造成稀釋 ，但今年資
本支出財測維持600到640億美元不變，反映
代理AI客戶需求強勁 ，預期2026年至2028年
資本支出將較過去三年進一步顯著提高。

資產同步下挫。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐

評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

### Page 8

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

投資焦點
近3個月漲幅和預估獲利呈正成長，醫療生技股佳
醫療保健和生技預估獲利明年明顯成長，有利股價續揚

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 345: OPECプラス、２ヵ月連続で生産目標据え置き ～Ｇ７は備蓄放出の方針も、原油市場は生産枠より中東の供給制約を注視する展開が続く～

OPEC プラス、２ヵ月連続で生産目標据え置き 
～Ｇ７は備蓄放出の方針も、原油市場は生産枠より中東の供給制約を注視する展開が続く～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ OPEC プラスの有志７ヵ国は10 月４日の会合で、11 月の原油生産目標を９月の水準に据え置
くことを決定し、２ヵ月連続で増産を見送った。有志国は今年４月以降、2023 年に表明した
自主減産を段階的に縮小し、９月にはその解消を完了したものの、その後は市場環境や加盟
国の生産状況を見極める姿勢に転じている。 
○ ただし、今回の据え置きは原油高を容認したものではなく、イラン情勢の混乱によって中東
産油国が生産目標を引き上げても実際の供給を十分に増やせないことが背景にある。有志７
ヵ国の８月の生産量は、イラン情勢悪化前の２月を日量約500 万バレル下回り、湾岸産油国
の輸出も平時の６～８割程度にとどまる。このため、これまでの増産措置は供給不足の緩和
につながらず、国際原油価格は高止まりしている。Ｇ７は原油高対策を目的に、備蓄放出に
動く方針を打ち出したが、需給逼迫の解消には中東からの供給回復が不可欠である。 
○ また、OPEC プラスの合同閣僚監視委員会は、 海上輸送ルートの確保やエネルギー関連インフ
ラへの攻撃に強い懸念を示しており、原油市場の焦点は生産枠そのものより、ホルムズ海峡
を中心とする輸送ルートや中東産油国の生産 ・ 輸出能力に移っている。 先行きもOPEC プラス
が早期に増産路線へ復帰する可能性は低く、当面の原油価格はホルムズ海峡の航行状況や中
東の供給正常化に大きく左右される展開が続くと予想される。

主要産油国の枠組みである OPEC プラスの有志７ヵ国（サウジアラビア、ロシア、イラク、クウ
ェート、 カザフスタン、 アルジェリア、 オマーン） は、10 月４日にオンラインで閣僚会合を開催し、
11 月の原油生産目標を９月の水準に据え置くことを決定した。９月６日に開催 した前回会合でも
10 月の生産目標を９月の水準に据え置いており（注１）、増産を見送るのは２ヵ月連続となる。有
志国は今後も毎月会合を開催して市場環境を点検し、次回会合は11 月１日に予定されている。 
有志国は、2023 年４月に表明した日量約165 万バレル規模の自主減産について、今年４月以降、

注１ ９月７日付レポート「OPEC 有志７ヵ国､自主減産終了後の10 月は様子見姿勢で合意」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/654829.html）

### Page 2

2/3 
段階的な縮小に動いてきた。特に７～９月は毎月日量 18.8 万バレルずつ生産目標を引き上げ、９
月には同自主減産の解消を完了した。一方、９月の会合では 10 月の生産目標を据え置く方針に転
じ、今回もその方針が維持された。 
OPEC プラスは、 世界的な景気や原油需要の動向など市場環境を見極めるとともに、 加盟国による
生産目標の順守状況にも目配りする姿勢を示している。もっとも、 今回の据え置き決定は原油高を
容認したと判断するのは適切ではないであろう。背景には、イラン情勢を巡る混乱により、中東産
油国が生産目標を引き上げても、 原油供給を十分に増やすことが難しい状況にあることが挙げられ
る。有志７ヵ国の８月の原油生産量は日量約 2,500 万バレルと、前月から 63 万バレル増加したも
のの、 イラン情勢が悪化する前の２月と比較して約 500 万バレル少ない水準にとどまった。 湾岸産
油国の原油輸出も、このところ平時の６～８割程度にとどまる状況が続いている。このため、これ
までの生産目標引き上げの多くは「絵に描いた餅」となり、原油市場の供給不足を十分に緩和する
には至っていない。 
こうした状況を反映して、 国際原油価格は高止まりしている（図１）。このため、原油高によるイ
ンフレ圧力や世界経済への悪影響が懸
念される。こうしたなか、Ｇ７（主要７
ヵ国）は９月２日、ＩＥＡ（国際エネル
ギー機関） を通じて原油や石油製品など
合計１億バレルを４ヵ月にわたって協
調放出する方針を表明した。 特に、供給
逼迫が深刻な軽油について、当初 20 日
間に前倒しで放出することで市場の安
定を図るとしている。 しかし、 有志７ヵ
国の生産量が２月と比較して日量約500
万バレル少ないことを勘案すれば、 備蓄
放出のみで需給逼迫を解消することは難しく、中東地域からの供給回復が不可欠である。 
さらに注目されるのが、 有志国会合と同日に開催されたOPEC プラスの合同閣僚監視委員会 （JMMC）
の声明である。JMMC は「国際的な海上輸送ルートを確保し、 エネルギーの流れを途絶させないこと
が極めて重要」との認識を示した。また、エネルギー関連インフラへの攻撃についても「損傷した
設備の復旧には多額の費用と時間を要し、 供給能力そのものに悪影響を与える」との懸念を示した。
これは、足元の原油市場における最大の問題が OPEC プラスの生産目標ではなく、ホルムズ海峡を
中心とする輸送ルートや中東産油国の生産・輸出能力に移っていることを示唆する。 
先行きについても、OPEC プラスが早期に増産路線に復帰する可能性は低いとみられる。OPEC プ
ラス全体では、なお日量約200 万バレル規模の減産措置が年末まで残されている。一方、加盟国の
実際の生産能力を精査する作業は、 イラン情勢に伴う混乱により遅れている模様である。生産能力
の評価は、2027 年以降の各国の生産基準を決定するうえで重要となるため、OPEC プラスとしても
目先の追加増産より、2027 年以降の生産体制の構築に軸足を移している可能性が高い。

このため、 当面の原油価格を左右する材料としては、OPEC プラスが生産枠をどの程度引き上げる
か以上に、ホルムズ海峡の航行状況や中東産油国の輸出回復が重要になる。有志国は 11 月１日に
12 月の生産方針を協議する予定であり、その後、11 月 29 日には JMMC も開催される。中東情勢の
先行きが見通しにくいなか、OPEC プラスにとっては、 原油価格の安定と世界市場への供給確保を両
立させる難しい政策運営が続くことになろう。

## Document 367: The Currency Book (2026-09-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Currency Book (2026-09-29).pdf

Introduction 
With trillions of dollars, euros and yen changing hands every day, foreign 
exchange is indisputably the largest and most liquid financial market in the 
world. Yet despite its size, the currency market is also one of the least 
understood. Unlike bonds or equities, there is no widely accepted framework for 
explaining currency moves, which reflect a complex interplay of 
macroeconomics, capital flows, market structure, investor behaviour and 
(geo)politics. 
Over the past two decades, advances in technology and market infrastructure 
have transformed how currencies are traded, placing FX at the forefront of 
broader changes in global finance. Yet because currencies sit at the intersection 
of national economies, the FX market is also fundamentally connected to 
international trade and cross-border capital flows. It is therefore increasingly 
exposed to the forces reshaping the global economic landscape—from 
geopolitical fragmentation and changing trade relationships to shifts in 
regulation, payments infrastructure and the architecture of the international 
monetary system. 
This Deutsche Bank Currency Book brings together academic, industry and 
Deutsche Bank expertise to explain how FX markets function, how exchange 
rates are determined, how investors seek to generate returns from currencies, 
and how FX risk can be managed by investors and corporates alike. Our aim 
throughout the Book is to set out the enduring principles driving markets 
alongside the developments defining FX today. Ultimately, the structure of the FX 
market is not simply a question of how currencies trade, but of who controls the 
financial infrastructure through which money moves across borders. The 
evolution of FX is increasingly intertwined with questions of monetary 
sovereignty, financial resilience and geopolitical power. 
Chapter 1 reviews the structure of the FX market, examining its participants, 
ecosystem and evolution over time. Chapter 2 discusses exchange rate 
determination, moving from traditional academic theories to modern macro-
financial approaches and practical decision frameworks. Chapter 3 reviews FX 
as an asset class and the evidence on currency investing, while Chapter 4 
considers currency hedging for both investors and corporates. Finally, Chapter 5 
discusses the future of FX, including the impact of technology, payment systems 
and digital assets.

Introduction
With trillions of dollars, euros and yen changing hands every day, foreign exchange
is indisputably the largest and most liquid financial market in the world. Yet despite
its size, the currency market is also one of the least understood. Unlike bonds or
equities, there is no widely accepted framework for explaining currency moves,
which reflect a complex  interplay of macroeconomics,  capital flows,  market
structure, investor behaviour and (geo)politics.

Over the past two decades, advances in technology and market infrastructure have
transformed how currencies are traded, placing FX at the  forefront of broader
changes in global finance. Yet because currencies sit at the intersection of national
economies, the FX market is also fundamentally connected to international trade
and cross-border capital flows. It is therefore increasingly exposed to the forces
reshaping the global economic landscape—from geopolitical fragmentation and

changing trade relationships to shifts in regulation, payments infrastructure and
the architecture of the international monetary system.
This Deutsche Bank Currency Book brings together  academic, industry and
Deutsche Bank expertise to explain how FX markets function, how exchange rates
are determined, how investors seek to generate returns from currencies, and how
FX risk can be managed by investors and corporates alike. Our aim throughout
the Book is to set out the enduring principles driving markets alongside

the developments defining FX today. Ultimately, the structure of the FX
market is not simply a question of how currencies trade, but of who
controls the financial infrastructure through which money moves across
borders. The evolution of FX is increasingly intertwined with questions
of monetary sovereignty, financial resilience and geopolitical power.

Chapter 1 reviews the structure of the FX market, examining its participants,

### Page 7

The Currency Book
Deutsche Bank AG Page 7
Introduction
The foreign exchange market is the world’s largest and most liquid financial market,
but it differs fundamentally from a centralised exchange. Trading takes place across
a fragmented, largely over-the-counter network of banks, non-bank liquidity
providers, electronic venues and end-users, using instruments ranging from spot
and forwards to swaps, futures and options. How do transactions move through

this network? Who provides liquidity, who consumes it and what risks arise
between execution and settlement? This chapter maps the FX ecosystem from
instruments and trading infrastructure to the participants ultimately responsible for
currency demand.
1.1 Instruments
The FX market is not one single product. Instead, it is made up of a group of
instruments that allow market participants to exchange currencies today, lock in an
exchange rate for the future and/or hedge against adverse exchange-rate moves.

Below, we outline the main instruments in FX markets and their users.
Spot: This is the simplest and most widely known FX instrument. When market
participants refer to “the exchange-rate” or the “spot exchange-rate,” they
generally mean the price agreed today for exchanging one currency for another on
the standard spot settlement date. For most major currency pairs spot settlement
is two business days later (T+2).1  For example, a spot purchase of EUR 1 million

against the USD at a EUR/USD exchange-rate of 1.20 means that the buyer receives
EUR 1 million in his or her bank account on the settlement date and the seller
receives USD 1.2 million from the buyer on the same date.
1 There are exceptions, for example USD/CAD settles T+1.

### Page 8

The Currency Book
Page 8 Deutsche Bank AG
By convention, the first currency in an exchange-rate quotation is known as the
base currency, while the second is the quote currency. The exchange-rate therefore
represents the number of units of the quote currency required to purchase one unit
of the base currency. In USD/JPY, for example, the USD is the base currency and the
exchange-rate indicates the number of Japanese yen required to purchase one US

dollar. A USD/JPY exchange-rate of 160 means 1 USD can be exchanged for 160
JPY.
Spot transactions are the most commonly used foreign exchange payments for
cross-border payments for goods and services, the purchase of foreign financial
assets, as well as portfolio rebalancing and proprietary trading. In 2025, the Bank
for International Settlements (BIS) estimates spot turnover at $3trn per day,
accounting for 31% of global over-the-counter (OTC) FX turnover.

Forwards and futures: An (outright) forward is similar to a spot transaction, but is
instead an agreement to exchange two currencies at a rate (price) agreed today, but
with settlement taking place at a date more than T+2 days in the future. They are
also traded OTC. Forwards are most commonly used by those who have a known
future cash flow and want to remove uncertainty around the future exchange rate.
In 2025, the BIS estimates that they account for 19% of global turnover, or $1.8trn

per day.
Futures are economically similar to forwards but are standardised contracts traded
on exchanges and centrally cleared. This provides transparent pricing and reduces
bilateral counterparty risk, but offers less flexibility over contract size, maturity and
settlement terms than an OTC forward. Exchange-traded FX futures remain
considerably smaller than the OTC FX market.
Non-deliverable forwards (NDFs): An NDF is a cash-settled forward commonly

used for currencies where convertibility or offshore deliverability is restricted. Two
parties agree today on an exchange rate for a future date, but unlike a standard
forward, the underlying currencies are not exchanged. At maturity, the agreed NDF
rate is compared with a pre-specified fixing or reference rate. The difference is
usually settled in USD. NDFs are commonly used by corporates, banks and
investors to hedge or take exposure to currencies subject to capital controls.

Swaps: An FX swap combines two transactions: an exchange of currencies on an
initial date and a reverse exchange at a later date, with both exchange rates agreed
when the swap is initiated. They are used by institutions that hold cash in one
currency (e.g. EUR) but need funding in another (e.g. USD) for investment, hedging
or other needs. They are by far the largest FX instrument by turnover, with the BIS
estimating turnover of $4trn per day in April 2025, 42% of global FX turnover. A

swap is, in effect, a form of collateralised borrowing, with the collateral being cash
in a different currency to that of the currency borrowed.
Cross-currency (XCCY) swaps: A cross-currency swap is similar to an FX swap in
that principal amounts denominated in two different currencies are typically
exchanged at inception and reversed at maturity. The key difference is that a cross-
currency swap also involves periodic exchanges of interest payments denominated

in the two currencies over the life of the contract. These payments may be fixed or
floating. Cross-currency swaps therefore allow market participants to transform
both the currency denomination and, where relevant, the interest-rate
characteristics of an asset or liability.

### Page 9

The Currency Book
Deutsche Bank AG Page 9
For example:
n Suppose a European corporate issues a USD-denominated bond to US-
based investors. It receives the bond proceeds in USD, but must
periodically pay interest in USD and repay the USD principal when the bond
matures. However, because the corporate primarily earns revenue in EUR,
it needs to convert these USD liabilities into EUR liabilities to avoid currency
and potentially interest-rate risk.

n The corporate removes FX and interest-rate risk by entering into a xccy
swap, where, in essence, it converts this USD-liability into a EUR-liability.2
n They are commonly used by banks, corporates and investors that need to
raise money in one currency but need funds in another. The basis is the extra
spread added to one side of the swap to balance unequal demand for
funding in the two currencies. See Section 2.11 for more on cross-currency
basis and here.

Options: An option gives the holder the right, but not the obligation, to buy or sell
one currency against another at a specified exchange rate during a specified period.
Options are used to hedge currency risk or speculate on currency movements and
are particularly useful when the end-user wants an asymmetric payoff profile.
Unlike a forward, an option can limit losses from an adverse currency movement
while allowing the holder to retain some or all of the benefit from a favourable

movement. In 2025, they represented around 7% of global FX turnover. Options are
widely used by corporates and investors for hedging, and by investors and dealers
to express directional or volatility views.
The simplest and most widely used instruments are vanilla options, which give the
holder the right to buy (a call) or sell (a put) a currency at a predetermined strike
price. A European-style option can be exercised only at expiry, while an American-

style option can be exercised at any point up to expiry. More structured products
include barrier options, whose payoff is activated or extinguished if the exchange
rate reaches a specified level—for example, knock-in and knock-out options. One-
touch and no-touch options pay a fixed amount depending on whether the
exchange-rate does, or does not, reach a predetermined level during the life of the
option. These instruments are commonly used to express views not just on the

direction of a currency pair, but also on the probability, timing and magnitude of
exchange-rate moves.
1.2 FX spot trading life cycle and risks
Having discussed the main FX instruments, the next step is to examine how these
transactions are executed and settled in practice. The complete trade life cycle,
meaning the sequence of events and processes involved in executing a trade, is
crucial to understanding how FX transactions work. We therefore describe below

the broad steps involved in trading FX, focusing on spot transactions. They are:
1.  Pre-trade: This stage encompasses all the necessary preparations before
a trade is executed, including client onboarding, platform selection, credit
checks, and regulatory compliance.
2 Specifically, it exchanges the USD bond proceeds for EUR at inception, receives USD interest payments
(from the swap counterparty to pay U.S. bond investors) and pays EUR interest (to the swap

counterparty) over the life of the swap, and receives USD principal (from the swap counterparty to pay
U.S. investors) in exchange for EUR principal at maturity. The USD receipts from the swap are used to
meet the bond’s USD interest and principal payments, effectively converting the USD-denominated
bond into a EUR-denominated liability.

### Page 10

The Currency Book
Page 10 Deutsche Bank AG
2.  Trade execution: This is the point where the trade is agreed upon and
executed at a specific exchange rate, creating a legally binding contract.
3.  Trade clearing: This stage involves validating trade details and managing
counterparty credit risk before settlement, either bilaterally or through a
central clearing counterparty (CCP). In centrally cleared FX markets, the
CCP becomes the buyer to every seller and the seller to every buyer,

reducing direct counterparty exposures between market participants.
Specialist FX CCPs, such as LCH's ForexClear, clear both non-deliverable
and deliverable FX products. While FX spot transactions can be cleared,
they are not subject to mandatory clearing requirements. ForexClear
supports 25 non-deliverable forward (NDF) currency pairs, 9 non-
deliverable option (NDO) currency pairs, and 8 deliverable (Spot, Forwards
and Options) currency pairs, at the time of writing.

4.  Trade settlement: This final stage represents the completion of the
transaction, with the actual exchange of currencies taking place.
Settlement for spot trades typically occurs two business days (T+2) after
the trade date, although exceptions exist. For example, USD/CAD settles
on a T+1 basis.
Figure 2: FX Spot Trade Life Cycle
Source : Deutsche Bank
Risks surrounding FX spot trading
The over-the-counter (OTC) nature of the FX market, while offering flexibility and

adaptability to participant needs, does present certain risks that are often more
pronounced compared to exchange-traded markets. Below we discuss some of the
key risks in FX.
Credit risk: Credit risk in FX trading, stemming from a counterparty's potential
inability to fulfill its obligations, is present throughout the trade lifecycle:
n Pre-trade: Counterparty creditworthiness is a critical consideration in the
A Central Clearing Counterparty (CCP), also

known as a Central Counterparty, stands
between buyers and sellers in financial
markets, becoming the buyer to every
seller and the seller to every buyer. This
mitigates counterparty risk.

### Page 11

The Currency Book
Deutsche Bank AG Page 11
pre-trade stage of FX transactions. A thorough assessment should involve
evaluating the potential counterparty's financial strength, credit rating, and
reputation. Choosing a creditworthy counterparty significantly reduces the
risk of default throughout the trade lifecycle.
n Post-trade (Clearing and Settlement): While credit risk exists in both the
clearing (post-execution/pre-settlement) and settlement stages of a spot

FX trade, it primarily materializes during settlement. This stage, involving
the actual exchange of currencies, carries the highest risk due to the
potential for settlement failure. This risk, often referred to as settlement risk,
arises when one party delivers their currency while the counterparty
defaults. Cross-border trades are particularly vulnerable due to time zone
differences, which can create a window where one party has performed but
the other has not. Additionally, mark-to-market risk from FX rate

fluctuations between trade and settlement dates can exacerbate
settlement risk by increasing potential losses from a default. This is
particularly pronounced with forward contracts, where the longer pre-
settlement period leads to greater mark-to-market risk and, consequently,
higher settlement risk.
Market Risk: This risk in FX trading arises from potential losses due to adverse
exchange-rate movements. This is influenced by factors like economic data,

geopolitical events, and interest rates. For customers, asymmetric information,
where one party has more or better information than another, can lead to inefficient
pricing and increased volatility, thereby exacerbating market risk.
n For dealers, a key component of market risk is inventory management risk
– the potential for losses from unfavourable price movements in the
currencies they hold to facilitate client trading. Effective inventory
management, including position limits and hedging, mitigates this risk.

Liquidity Risk: This refers to the overall market's ability to facilitate the buying and
selling of a currency pair quickly and efficiently at a desired price. It's influenced by
factors such as market depth, trading volume, volatility, and fragmentation.
Operational Risk: This encompasses risks associated with trade processing,
settlement, and technology. Errors in trade execution, system failures, and fraud
can all lead to significant losses.
Regulatory Risk: This refers to the risk arising from changes in regulations or their

interpretation. The evolving regulatory landscape in FX, particularly concerning
non-bank dealers and systemic risk, can impact market participants and create
uncertainty.
1.3 Market participants
The FX market comprises a variety of participants with different objectives. FX
market participants fall into two broad categories: financial and non-financial.
Financial participants can act as both liquidity providers and consumers, while non-
financial participants act largely as liquidity consumers.

Financial participants: In the FX market, financial participants are primarily banks
acting as dealers.
n Dealers: These are mainly large commercial banks, investment banks and
securities houses that participate in the inter-dealer market for proprietary

### Page 12

The Currency Book
Page 12 Deutsche Bank AG
trading. 3 and/or for meeting customer demand through market-making.4
n Institutional investors: These are mutual funds, pension funds, insurance
companies and endowment funds, collectively referred to as “real-money
investors”, which need to buy currencies to satisfy end-user demand for an
underlying asset. For instance, a UK pension fund needs to buy USD to
purchase US equities. These investors also engage in hedging, proprietary

trading and risk management activities.
n Hedge Funds & Principal Trading Firms (PTFs): Hedge funds are
investment funds that follow a broad range of investment strategies and are
generally not subject to the same borrowing and leverage restrictions as
traditional investment funds. They are users of FX and derivatives for
speculative purposes. PTFs are principal trading firms, such as XTX
Markets and Citadel Securities, that hedge or speculate for their own

account and also participate in market-making. This category may include
high-frequency trading firms that employ high-speed algorithmic trading
strategies.
Non financial institutions: Non-financial participants, such as corporations
involved in international trade, retail and the official sector, also primarily consume
liquidity.
n Corporates are active in currency markets primarily to manage FX
exposures 5  arising from international trade, foreign-currency financing,

overseas operations and cross-border mergers and acquisitions (M&A).6
n Official sector: This category includes central banks, sovereign wealth
funds, international financial institutions, such as the IMF and BIS,
development banks and government agencies. Monetary-policy, financial-
stability and mercantilist considerations have all, at various times,
motivated central-bank intervention in FX.7
n Other: This category includes all other market participants, including retail

participants. Retail participants engage in FX both to speculate and to meet
transactional demand arising from travel, trade or investment.
1.4 Profit versus non-profit seekers
In 2025, daily FX turnover reached $9.5 trillion according to the BIS triennial survey
(BIS, 2025) making currency trading the world's largest financial market. Yet,
size and liquidity do not necessarily imply efficiency. Unlike most equity or
bond transactions, a large share of FX activity serves an underlying

transactional purpose. For instance, corporates must convert revenues and
meet foreign- currency invoices; institutional investors hedge overseas
assets; and dealers intermediate these flows. For these participants,
expected FX returns may be secondary, or even irrelevant, to the decision to
trade. In this section, we investigate
3 Proprietary trading is a profit-seeking activity that dealers once engaged in more extensively, but which
has been severely constrained by regulatory change. Most notably, the Volcker Rule, implemented in

2014-15 under the Dodd-Frank regulations, bans banks from engaging in proprietary trading activities,
including many foreign exchange instruments.
4 Market making: This is an activity that seeks to capture returns by matching buyers and sellers and
charging a price for the service through the bid-offer spread. This spread represents the price that
liquidity seekers must pay to access the market and is a “frictional” cost of executing transactions. As

such, market-making is a liquidity-provision service rather than a source of the ‘excess returns'
associated with activities such as proprietary trading

(2) Mandate constraints relate to limits

constraints on investment allocations and
government policy (i.e. Norway's pension

and therefore being explicitly required to

(3) Process constraints relate to limits

Approach 1: The Triennial BIS Survey of FX market volumes
We use the 2025 BIS Triennial Survey to update Deutsche Bank's previous work

(Saravelos et al, 2018) and estimate the share of FX turnover initiated with a profit-
seeking currency objective. We construct a "realistic scenario", which applies what
we deem to be "realistic" assumptions about participants’ ability and mandate to
take FX risk.8 We find that profit-seeking activity accounts for approximately 40%
of turnover under the realistic scenario.9
Financial participants
n Dealers: Dealer-to-dealer activity accounts for approximately 47% of total

daily FX turnover ($4.44trn) and we classify 10% of dealer flow as profit-
seeking ($444bn/day). Given that much of the daily FX turnover reflects
market-making, we assume that inter-dealer transactions conducted by
voice are not profit-seeking and that only electronic transactions
conducted through order-matching systems such as EBS are profit-
seeking.
n Hedge Funds and PTFs: Hedge Funds and PTFs account for approximately
$760bn (8%) of headline daily FX turnover and we classify 90% of this as

profit-seeking ($684bn/day). The $684bn/day figure represents the profit-
seeking nature of hedge-funds where the remaining 10% reflects the
ensure that the mark-to-market value of their hedge does not introduce unwanted volatility in the
income statement. IFRS 9 provided an updated set of standards for hedge accounting, designed to
increase flexibility and better link financial reporting to firms’ risk management practices. However,
hedge accounting still places limitations on the types of instruments and kinds of hedges that can be

implemented. For example, to qualify for hedge accounting, corporates must adhere to hedge
effectiveness and demonstrate that an economic relationship between the underlying cash flow or
asset and the hedge exists, as well as quantitative limits to hedge ratios.
7 Exchange rates are explicitly targeted by some central banks as part of their overall monetary policy
framework (for instance the Monetary Authority of Singapore or the Hong Kong Monetary Authority);

other central banks have intervened to defend their inflation target or financial stability reasons (most
recently, the Swiss National Bank); and others to prevent competitiveness losses in global trade.

expansion of PTFs to act not only as profit-seekers but also as liquidity
providers.

$2.19trn, or 23% of headline daily turnover and we classify 10% of this as
profit-seeking ($220bn/day). These largely intermediate for institutional

and non-financial customers and apply the same profit-seeking

profit-seeking share of approximately 30%.

Approach 2: Commitment of Traders CFTC data
While the BIS data provide the most comprehensive view of the global FX market,
the survey is only updated every three years. For a higher-frequency perspective, we
therefore complement it with the US Commodity Futures Trading Commission’s
Commitments of Traders (CoT) report, which is published weekly and provides a

breakdown of positions in exchange-traded currency futures and options,
principally those traded on the Chicago Mercantile Exchange (CME). The report
sho
ws the number of outstanding long and short contracts held by different
categories of market participant, based on positions as of each Tuesday. Although
the futures market represents only a small subset of total global FX activity—most
of which takes place over the counter—the CoT data provide a timely and consistent

measure of how speculative and hedging positions are evolving.
11 Corporates use FX to settle foreign-currency revenues and expenses, finance cross-border activity,
hedge forecast cash flows and manage earnings volatility. Governments similarly transact to meet an
underlying funding or expenditure requirement.
12 Some investors trade currencies to earn returns, while other transactions relate to travel, remittances
or purchases of foreign assets

### Page 16

The Currency Book
Page 16 Deutsche Bank AG
In the traditional or “legacy” CoT report, reportable positions  are divided into
commercial and non-commercial traders, alongside a residual category of smaller,
non-reportable positions. Commercial traders are generally entities that use futures
primarily in connection  with an underlying business activity  and are therefore
commonly interpreted as hedgers—for  example, corporates or financial
institutions managing currency exposures. We classify these positions as liquidity

seeking. Non-commercial traders,  by contrast, are participants  whose futures
activity is not classified as commercial hedging and typically includes hedge funds,
commodity trading advisers and other leveraged investors. We therefore treat
non-commercial positions as profit-seeking. Figure 7 and Figure 8 show how the
relative size of the profit-seeking FX market has developed over time. Using one-
year rolling averages to reduce the noise of the data, we see that roughly 30-40%

of the futures market (EUR/USD, GBP/USD, USD/JPY) is profit-seeking.
